- **ste**: ASD-STE100 Simplified Technical English for Claude Code. A rule injected at session start governs every word Claude writes: replies, commit messages, comments, and documentation. A skill adds document structure when Claude writes docs. A PostToolUse linter checks every Markdown write mechanically, blocks on what a regular expression can decide, and only advises where the check is a heuristic. Each layer owns a different scope and none repeats another. /ste:adopt brings an existing repository under the same rules, one document at a time. ([dominik-rehse/ste](https://github.com/dominik-rehse/ste) · 2 skills, 1 hook file)
